[P1444] Detailed configuration for the transmitter characteristic tests are defined in TS 36.141 [24],
[P1445] 4.9.3	NB-IoT testing
[P1446] Unless otherwise stated, the NB-IoT operation in NR in-band test shall be performed by puncturing one NR RB at the eligible (according to clause 5.7.3 of TS 36.104 [22] and the definition in clause 3.1) in-band position closest to NR minimum guard band; those are denoted LNB-IoT (Left) and RNB-IoT (Right).
[P1447] Unless otherwise stated, the NB-IoT operation in NR in-band receiver tests shall be performed by using the tone located on the NB-IoT RB's edge, which is closest to NR minimum guard band; those are denoted BNB-IoT for LNB-IoT and TNB-IoT for RNB-IoT.
[P1448] Unless otherwise stated, a BS declared to be capable of NB-IoT operation in NR in-band is only required to pass the transmitter tests for NR with NB-IoT operation in NR in-band; it is not required to perform the transmitter tests again for NR only.
[P1449] Unless otherwise stated, a BS declared to be capable of NB-IoT operation in NR in-band is only required to pass the receiver tests for NR with NB-IoT operation in NR in-band; it is not required to perform the receiver tests again for NR only.
[P1450] NOTE:	The BS should be configured (RRC signalling) for the NB-IoT E-UTRA guardband mode during the tests.
[P1451] 4.10	Requirements for contiguous and non-contiguous spectrum
[P1452] A spectrum allocation where a BS operates can either be contiguous or non-contiguous. Unless otherwise stated, the requirements in the present specification apply for BS configured for both contiguous spectrum operation and non-contiguous spectrum operation.
[P1453] For BS operation in non-contiguous spectrum, some requirements apply both at the Base Station RF Bandwidth edges and inside the sub-block gaps. For each such requirement, it is stated how the limits apply relative to the Base Station RF Bandwidth edges and the sub-block edges respectively.
[P1454] There are no CA requirements for ATG BS in the present specification.
[P1455] 4.11	Requirements for BS capable of multi-band operation
[P1456] For multi-band connector the conducted test requirements in clause 6 and 7 apply separately to each supported operating band unless otherwise stated. For some conducted test requirements, it is explicitly stated that specific additions or exclusions to the requirement apply at multi-band connector(s) as detailed in the requirement clause. For BS capable of multi-band operation, various structures in terms of combinations of different transmitter and receiver implementations (multi-band or single band) with mapping of transceivers to one or more antenna connectors for BS type 1-C or TAB connectors for BS type 1-H in different ways are possible. For multi-band connector(s) the exclusions or provisions for multi-band apply. For single-band connector(s), the following applies:
[P1457] -	Single-band transmitter spurious emissions, operating band unwanted emissions, ACLR, transmitter intermodulation and receiver spurious emissions requirements apply to this connector that is mapped to single-band. In case there are carrier(s) transmitted simultaneously in another supported operating band in common active RF components, when the RF signals of these antenna connectors cover the same geographical area, the frequency range of the Base Station RF bandwidth in the other supported band to this antenna connector should be excluded from the unwanted emission requirements.
[P1458] -	If the BS is configured for single-band operation, single-band requirements shall apply to this connector configured for single-band operation and no exclusions or provisions for multi-band capable BS are applicable. Single-band requirements are tested separately at the connector configured for single-band operation, with all other connectors terminated.
[P1459] A BS type 1-H may be capable of supporting operation in multiple operating bands with one of the following implementations of TAB connectors in the transceiver array boundary:
[P1460] -	All TAB connectors are single-band connectors.
[P1461] -	Different sets of single-band connectors support different operating bands, but each TAB connector supports only operation in one single operating band.
[P1462] -	Sets of single-band connectors support operation in multiple operating bands with some single-band connectors supporting more than one operating band.
[P1463] -	All TAB connectors are multi-band connectors.
[P1464] -	A combination of single-band sets and multi-band sets of TAB connectors provides support of the type BS type 1-H capability of operation in multiple operating bands.
[P1465] Unless otherwise stated all conducted test requirements specified for an operating band apply only to the set of TAB connectors supporting that operating band.
[P1466] In the case of an operating band being supported only by single-band connectors in a TAB connector TX min cell group or a TAB connector RX min cell group, single-band requirements apply to that set of TAB connectors.
[P1467] In the case of an operating band being supported only by multi-band connectors supporting the same operating band combination in a TAB connector TX min cell group or a TAB connector RX min cell group, multi-band requirements apply to that set of TAB connectors.
[P1468] There is no multi-band operation requirement for ATG BS.
[P1469] For multi-band connectors supporting the bands for TDD, the RF requirements in the present specification assume no simultaneous uplink and downlink occur between the bands.
[P1470] NOTE 1: The case of an operating band being supported by both multi-band connectors and single-band connectors in a TAB connector TX min cell group or a TAB connector RX min cell group is not covered by the present release of this specification.
[P1471] NOTE 2: The case of an operating band being supported by multi-band connectors which are not all supporting the same operating band combination in a TAB connector TX min cell group or a TAB connector RX min cell group is not covered by the present release of this specification.
[P1472] NOTE 3: The conducted test requirements for multi-band connectors supporting bands for both FDD and TDD are not covered by the present release of this specification.
[P1473] 4.12	Format and interpretation of tests
[P1474] Each test has a standard format:
[P1475] X	Title
[P1476] All tests are applicable to all equipment within the scope of the present document, unless otherwise stated.
[P1477] X.1	Definition and applicability
[P1478] This clause gives the general definition of the parameter under consideration and specifies whether the test is applicable to all equipment or only to a certain subset. Required manufacturer declarations may be included here.
[P1479] X.2	Minimum requirement
[P1480] This clause contains the reference to the clause to the 3GPP reference (or core) specification which defines the minimum requirement.
[P1481] X.3	Test purpose
[P1482] This clause defines the purpose of the test.
[P1483] X.4	Method of test
[P1484] X.4.1	General
[P1485] In some cases there are alternative test procedures or initial conditions. In such cases, guidance for which initial conditions and test procedures can be applied are stated here. In the case only one test procedure is applicable, that is stated here.
[P1486] X.4.2y	First test method
[P1487] X.4.2y.1	Initial conditions
[P1488] This clause defines the initial conditions for each test, including the test environment, the RF channels to be tested and the basic measurement set-up.
[P1489] X.4.2y.2	Procedure
[P1490] This clause describes the steps necessary to perform the test and provides further details of the test definition like domain (e.g. frequency-span), range, weighting (e.g. bandwidth), and algorithms (e.g. averaging). The procedure may comprise data processing of the measurement result before comparison with the test requirement (e.g. average result from several measurement positions).
[P1491] X.4.3y	Alternative test method (if any)
[P1492] If there are alternative test methods, each is described with its initial conditions and procedures.
[P1493] X.5	Test requirement
[P1494] This clause defines the pass/fail criteria for the equipment under test, see clause 4.1.3 (Interpretation of measurement results). Test requirements for every minimum requirement referred in clause X.2 are listed here. Cases where minimum requirements do not apply need not be mentioned.
[P1496] 5	Operating bands and channel arrangement
[P1497] For the NR and NB-IoT operation in NR in-band operating bands specification, their channel bandwidth configurations, channel spacing and raster, as well as synchronization raster specification, refer to TS 38.104 [2], clause 5 and its relevant clauses.
[P1498] For ATG BS, their operating bands refer to TS 38.104 [2].
[P1499] For the conducted testing purposes in this specification, only FR1 operating bands are considered.
[P1501] 6	Conducted transmitter characteristics
[P1502] 6.1	General
[P1503] 6.1.1	BS type 1-C
[P1504] General test conditions for conducted transmitter tests are given in clause 4, including interpretation of measurement results and configurations for testing. BS configurations for the tests are defined in clause 4.5.
[P1505] If a number of single-band connectors, or multi-band connectors have been declared equivalent (D.32), only a representative one is necessary to be tested to demonstrate conformance.
[P1506] 6.1.2	BS type 1-H
[P1507] General test conditions for conducted transmitter tests are given in clause 4, including interpretation of measurement results and configurations for testing. BS configurations for the tests are defined in clause 4.5.
[P1508] If a number of single-band connectors, or multi-band connectors have been declared equivalent (D.32), only a representative one is necessary to be tested to demonstrate conformance.
[P1509] In clause 6.6.3.5.4, if representative TAB connectors are used then per connector criteria (i.e. option 2) shall be applied.
[P1510] The manufacturer shall declare the minimum number of supported geographical cells (i.e. geographical areas). The minimum number of supported geographical cells (Ncells, D.24) relates to the BS setting with the minimum amount of cell splitting supported with transmission on all TAB connectors supporting the operating band. The manufacturer shall also declare TAB connector TX min cell groups (D.34). Every TAB connector supporting transmission in an operating band shall map to one TAB connector TX min cell group supporting the same operating band. The mapping of TAB connectors to cells is implementation dependent.
[P1511] The number of active transmitter units that are considered when calculating the emissions limit (NTXU, counted) for BS type 1-H is calculated as follows:
[P1512] NTXU, counted = min(NTXU,active, 8·Ncells)
[P1513] Further:
[P1514] NTXU,countedpercell = NTXU,counted/Ncells
[P1515] NTXU,countedpercell is used for scaling the basic limits as described in clause 6.6.
[P1516] NOTE:	NTXU,active depends on the actual number of active transmitter units and is independent to the declaration of Ncells.
[P1517] For BS type 1-H there is no requirement specified for band n46, n100, n101 and n102.
[P1518] 6.2	Base station output power
[P1519] 6.2.1	Definition and applicability
[P1520] The conducted BS output power requirements are specified at single-band connector, or at multi-band connector.
[P1521] The rated carrier output power of the BS type 1-C shall be as specified in table 6.2.1-1.
[P1522] Table 6.2.1-1: Rated carrier output power limits for BS type 1-C
[P1524] For operation in bands n100 and n101 in CEPT countries subject to the ECC Decision (20)02 [21], the WA BS requirement in table 6.2.1-1 apply in case of coordinated RMR BS deployments, while the requirements in clause 6.2.4 apply in case of uncoordinated RMR BS deployments.
[P1525] The rated carrier output power of the BS type 1-H shall be as specified in table 6.2.1-2.
[P1526] Table 6.2.1-2: Rated carrier output power limits for BS type 1-H
[P1528] In addition, for operation with shared spectrum channel access operation, the BS may have to comply with the applicable BS power limits established regionally, when deployed in regions where those limits apply and under the conditions declared by the manufacturer.
[P1529] For Band n41 and n90 operation in Japan, the rated output power, Prated,c.sys for BS type 1-H or the sum of Prated,c,AC over all antenna connectors for BS type 1-C declared by the manufacturer shall be equal to or less than 20 W per 10 MHz bandwidth.
[P1530] For band n100 in CEPT countries subject to the ECC Decision (20)02 [21], Prated,c,AC shall not exceed 51.5 dBm/5MHz + (fDL-922.1) x 40/3 dB, with fDL being the centre frequency in MHz. This limit is derived from ECC Decision (20)02 [25] assuming a 17 dBi maximum antenna gain and 4dB losses, and assuming one antenna connector. The above rated output power limit for band n100 applies to uncoordinated deployments and in case of coordinated deployments, higher output power values may be allowed.
[P1531] NOTE 1:	The additional maximum output power requirement for 3 MHz channel bandwidth will be updated in a later version, based on CEPT WG FM’s feedback.
[P1532] For CEPT countries subject to the ECC Decision (20)02 [21], administrations wishing to allow multiple carriers, i.e., more than one wideband carrier (LTE, NR, NB-IoT standalone, or NR/LTE with NB-IoT in-band) in band n100, should consider the implementation of a coordination procedure or other mitigation measures.
[P1533] NOTE 2:	Standalone NB-IoT operation is specified in TS 36.104 [22].
[P1534] For band n101 in CEPT countries subject to the ECC Decision (20)02 [21], Prated,c,AC shall not exceed 51 dBm/10MHz or 48 dBm/5MHz. This limit is derived from ECC Decision (20)02 [25] assuming a 18 dBi maximum antenna gain and 4dB losses, and assuming one antenna connector. The above rated output power limit for band n101 applies to uncoordinated deployments and in case of coordinated deployments, higher output power values may be allowed.
[P1535] For CEPT countries subject to the ECC Decision (20)02 [21], administrations wishing to allow multiple carriers, i.e., more than one wideband carrier (LTE, NR, NB-IoT standalone, or NR/LTE with NB-IoT in-band) in band n101, should consider the implementation of a coordination procedure or other mitigation measures.
[P1536] NOTE 3:	Standalone NB-IoT operation is specified in TS 36.104 [22].
[P1537] The output power limit for the respective BS classes in tables 6.2.1.-1 and 6.2.1-2 shall be compared to the rated output power and the declared BS class. It is not subject to testing.
[P1538] 6.2.2	Minimum requirement
[P1539] The minimum requirement applies per single-band connector, or per multi-band connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1540] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C is defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.2.2.
[P1541] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-H is defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.2.3.
[P1542] 6.2.3	Test purpose
[P1543] The test purpose is to verify the accuracy of the maximum carrier output power across the frequency range and under normal and extreme conditions.
[P1544] 6.2.4	Method of test
[P1545] 6.2.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1546] Test environment:
[P1547] -	Normal, see annex B.2,
[P1548] -	Extreme, see annexes B.3 and B.5.
[P1549] RF channels to be tested for single carrier:	B, M and T; see clause 4.9.1
[P1550] Base Station RF Bandwidth positions to be tested for multi-carrier and/or CA:
[P1551] -	BRFBW, MRFBW and TRFBW for single-band connector(s), see clause 4.9.1.
[P1552] -	BRFBW_T'RFBW and B'RFBW_TRFBW for multi-band connector(s), see clause 4.9.1.
[P1553] Under extreme test environment, it is sufficient to test on  one NR-ARFCN or one RF bandwidth position, and with  one applicable test configuration defined in clauses 4.7 and 4.8. Testing shall be performed under extreme power supply conditions, as defined in Annex B.5.
[P1554] NOTE:	Tests under extreme power supply conditions also test extreme temperatures.
[P1555] 6.2.4.2	Procedure
[P1556] For BS type 1-H where there may be multiple TAB connectors, they may be tested one at a time or multiple TAB connectors may be tested in parallel as shown in annex D.3.1. Whichever method is used the procedure is repeated until all TAB connectors necessary to demonstrate conformance have been tested.
[P1557] 1)	Connect the power measuring equipment to single-band connector(s) or to multi-band connector(s) under test as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C for in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. All connectors not under test shall be terminated.
[P1558] 2)	For single carrier set the connector under test to transmit according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 using the corresponding test models or set of physical channels in clause 4.9.2 at rated carrier output power Prated,c,AC for BS type 1-C and Prated,c,TABC for BS type 1-H (D.21).
[P1559] For a connector under test declared to be capable of multi-carrier and/or CA operation (D.15-D.16) set the connector under test to transmit on all carriers configured using the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8 using the corresponding test models or set of physical channels in clause 4.9.2.
[P1560] 3)	Measure the maximum carrier output power (Pmax,c,AC for BS type 1-C and Pmax,c,TABC for BS type 1-H) for each carrier at each connector under test.
[P1561] In addition, for multi-band connectors, the following steps shall apply:
[P1562] 4)	For a multi-band connectors and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved operating band where single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other operating band.
[P1563] 6.2.5	Test requirement
[P1564] For each single-band connector or multi-band connector under test, the power measured in clause 6.2.4.2 in step 3 shall remain within the values provided in table 6.2.5-1 for normal and extreme test environments, relative to the manufacturer's declared Prated,c,AC for BS type 1-C, or relative to the manufacturer's declared Prated,c,TABC for BS type 1-H (D.21):
[P1565] Table 6.2.5-1: Test requirement for conducted BS output power
[P1567] NOTE:	For NB-IoT operation in NR in-band, the NR carrier and NB-IoT carrier shall be seen as a single carrier occupied NR channel bandwidth, the output power over this carrier is shared between NR and NB-IoT. This note shall apply for Pmax,c,AC and Prated,c,AC.
[P1568] 6.3	Output power dynamics
[P1569] 6.3.1	General
[P1570] The requirements in clause 6.3 apply during the transmitter ON period. Transmit signal quality requirements (as specified in clause 6.5) shall be maintained for the output power dynamics requirements of this clause.
[P1571] 6.3.2	RE power control dynamic range
[P1572] 6.3.2.1	Definition and applicability
[P1573] The RE power control dynamic range is the difference between the power of an RE and the average RE power for a BS at maximum carrier output power (Pmax,c,TABC, or Pmax,c,AC) for a specified reference condition.
[P1574] 6.3.2.2	Minimum requirement
[P1575] The minimum requirement applies per single-band connector, or per multi-band connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1576] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C and for BS type 1-H is defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.3.2.2.
[P1577] 6.3.2.3	Test purpose
[P1578] No specific test or test requirements are defined for conducted RE power control dynamic range. The Error Vector Magnitude (EVM) test, as described in clause 6.5.3 provides sufficient test coverage for this requirement.
[P1579] 6.3.3	Total power dynamic range
[P1580] 6.3.3.1	Definition and applicability
[P1581] The BS total power dynamic range is the difference between the maximum and the minimum transmit power of an OFDM symbol for a specified reference condition.
[P1582] NOTE 1:	The upper limit of the total power dynamic range is the OFDM symbol TX power (OSTP) for a BS at maximum output power when transmitting on all RBs. The lower limit of the total power dynamic range is the average power for single RB transmission. The OFDM symbols shall carry PDSCH and not contain PDCCH, RS, or SSB.
[P1583] NOTE 2:	The requirement does not apply to operation with shared spectrum channel access.
[P1584] 6.3.3.2	Minimum requirement
[P1585] The minimum requirement applies per single-band connector, or per multi-band connector.
[P1586] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C and for BS type 1-H is in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.3.3.2.
[P1587] 6.3.3.3	Test purpose
[P1588] The test purpose is to verify that the total power dynamic range is within the limits specified by the minimum requirement.
[P1589] 6.3.3.4	Method of test
[P1590] 6.3.3.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1591] Test environment: Normal, see annex B.2.
[P1592] RF channels to be tested:	M; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1593] Set the channel set-up of the connector under as shown in annex D.1 for BS type 1-C and annex D.3 for BS type 1-H.
[P1594] 6.3.3.4.2	Procedure
[P1595] For BS type 1-H where there may be multiple TAB connectors, they may be tested one at a time or multiple TAB connectors may be tested in parallel as shown in annex D.3.1. Whichever method is used the procedure is repeated until all TAB connectors necessary to demonstrate conformance have been tested.
[P1596] 1)	Connect the single-band connector(s) under test as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C and in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. All connectors not under test shall be terminated.
[P1597] 2)	Set each connector under test to transmit according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 using the corresponding test models in clause 4.9.2 at rated carrier output power Prated,c,AC for BS type 1-C and Prated,c,TABC for BS type 1-H (D.21).
[P1598] 3)	For BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H, set the BS to transmit a signal according to:
[P1599] -	NR-FR1-TM3.1b if 1024QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1600] -	NR-FR1-TM3.1a if 1024QAM is not supported by BS without power back off but 256QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1601] -	NR-FR1-TM3.1 if 1024QAM and 256QAM are both not supported by BS without power back off.
[P1602] 4)	Measure the OFDM symbol TX power (OSTP) as defined in the annex H.
[P1603] 5)	For BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H, set the BS to transmit a signal according to:
[P1604] -	NR-FR1-TM2b if 1024QAM is supported by BS, or
[P1605] -	NR-FR1-TM2a if 1024QAM is not supported by BS and 256QAM is supported;
[P1606] -	NR-FR1-TM2 if 1024QAM and 256QAM are both not supported;
[P1607] 6)	Measure the OFDM symbol TX power (OSTP) as defined in the annex H.
[P1608] In addition, for multi-band connectors, the following steps shall apply:
[P1609] 7)	For a multi-band connectors and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved operating band where single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other operating band.
[P1610] 6.3.3.5	Test requirements
[P1611] The downlink (DL) total power dynamic range for each NR carrier shall be larger than or equal to the level in table 6.3.4.5-1.
[P1612] Table 6.3.3.5-1: BS total power dynamic range
[P1614] NOTE:	Additional test requirements for the EVM at the lower limit of the dynamic range are defined in clause 6.5.4.
[P1615] 6.3.4	NB-IoT RB power dynamic range
[P1616] 6.3.4.1	Definition and applicability
[P1617] The NB-IoT RB power dynamic range (or NB-IoT power boosting) is the difference between the average power of NB-IoT REs (which occupy certain REs within a NR transmission bandwidth configuration plus 15 kHz at each edge but not within the NR minimum guard band GBChannel) and the average power over all REs (from both NB-IoT and the NR carrier containing the NB-IoT REs).
[P1618] 6.3.4.2	Minimum requirement
[P1619] The minimum requirement applies per single-band connector, or per multi-band connector.
[P1620] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C is in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.3.4.2.
[P1621] 6.3.4.3	Test purpose
[P1622] The test purpose is to verify that the NB-IoT RB power dynamic range for NB-IoT operation in NR in-band is met as specified by the minimum requirement.
[P1623] 6.3.4.4	Method of test
[P1624] Requirement is tested together with operating band unwanted emissions test, as described in clause 6.6.4.
[P1625] 6.3.4.5	Test requirements
[P1626] NB-IoT RB power dynamic range for NB-IoT operation in NR in-band shall be larger than or equal to the level specified in Table 6.3.4.5-1 or the NB-IoT power dynamic range declared by the BS manufacturer (D.43). This power dynamic range level is only required for one NB-IoT RB.
[P1627] Table 6.3.4.5-1: NB-IoT RB power dynamic range for NB-IoT operation in NR in-band
[P1629] 6.4	Transmit ON/OFF power
[P1630] 6.4.1	Transmitter OFF power
[P1631] 6.4.1.1	Definition and applicability
[P1632] Transmit OFF power requirements apply only to TDD operation of the BS.
[P1633] Transmitter OFF power is defined as the mean power measured over 70/N us filtered with a square filter of bandwidth equal to the transmission bandwidth configuration of the BS (BWConfig) centred on the assigned channel frequency during the transmitter OFF period. N = SCS/15, where SCS is Sub Carrier Spacing in kHz.
[P1634] For multi-band connectors and for single band connectors supporting transmission in multiple operating bands, the requirement is only applicable during the transmitter OFF period in all supported operating bands.
[P1635] For BS supporting intra-band contiguous CA, the transmitter OFF power is defined as the mean power measured over 70/N us filtered with a square filter of bandwidth equal to the aggregated BS channel bandwidth BWChannel_CA centred on (Fedge_high+Fedge_low)/2 during the transmitter OFF period. N = SCS/15, where SCS is the smallest supported Sub Carrier Spacing in kHz in the aggregated BS channel bandwidth.
[P1636] 6.4.1.2	Minimum requirement
[P1637] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C is in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.4.1.2.
[P1638] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-H is in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.4.1.3.
[P1639] 6.4.1.3	Test purpose
[P1640] The purpose of this test is to verify the transmitter OFF power is within the limits of the minimum requirements.
[P1641] 6.4.1.4	Method of test
[P1642] Requirement is tested together with transmitter transient period, as described in clause 6.4.2.4.
[P1643] 6.4.1.5	Test requirements
[P1644] The conformance testing of transmit OFF power is included in the conformance testing of transmitter transient period; therefore, see clause 6.4.2.5 for test requirements.
[P1645] 6.4.2	Transmitter transient period
[P1646] 6.4.2.1	Definition and applicability
[P1647] Transmitter transient period requirements apply only to TDD operation of the BS.
[P1648] The transmitter transient period is the time period during which the transmitter unit is changing from the OFF period to the ON period or vice versa. The transmitter transient period is illustrated in figure 6.4.2.1-1.
[P1650] Figure 6.4.2.1-1: Illustration of the relations of transmitter ON period, transmitter OFF period and transmitter transient period
[P1651] This requirement applies at each antenna connector or TAB connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1652] 6.4.2.2	Minimum requirement
[P1653] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H is in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.4.2.2.
[P1654] 6.4.2.3	Test purpose
[P1655] The purpose of this test is to verify the transmitter transient periods are within the limits of the minimum requirements.
[P1656] 6.4.2.4	Method of test
[P1657] 6.4.2.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1658] Test environment:
[P1659] -	normal; see annex B.2.
[P1660] RF channels to be tested for single carrier:
[P1661] -	M; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1662] RF bandwidth positions to be tested for multi-carrier and/or CA:
[P1663] -	MRFBW in single-band operation, see clause 4.9.1;
[P1664] -	BRFBW_T'RFBW and B'RFBW_TRFBW in multi-band operation, see clause 4.9.1.
[P1665] 6.4.2.4.2	Procedure
[P1666] The minimum requirement is applied to all antenna connectors or TAB connectors, they may be tested one at a time or multiple antenna connectors or TAB connectors may be tested in parallel as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C or in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. Whichever method is used the procedure is repeated until all antenna connectors or TAB connectors necessary to demonstrate conformance have been tested.
[P1667] 1)	Connect antenna connector or TAB connector to measurement equipment as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C or in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. All antenna connectors or TAB connectors not under test shall be terminated.
[P1668] As a general rule, the resolution bandwidth of the measuring equipment should be equal to the measurement bandwidth. However, to improve measurement accuracy, sensitivity, efficiency and avoiding e.g. carrier leakage, the resolution bandwidth may be smaller than the measurement bandwidth. When the resolution bandwidth is smaller than the measurement bandwidth, the result should be integrated over the measurement bandwidth in order to obtain the equivalent noise bandwidth of the measurement bandwidth.
[P1669] 2)	For single carrier set the antenna connector or TAB connector under test to transmit according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 using the corresponding test models or set of physical channels in clause 4.9.2 at manufacturers declared rated carrier output power per antenna connector or TAB connector (Prated,c,AC, or Prated,c,TABC, D.21).
[P1670] For a connector under test declared to be capable of multi-carrier and/or CA operation (D.15-D.16) set the connector under test to transmit on all carriers configured using the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8 using the corresponding test models or set of physical channels in clause 4.9.2.
[P1671] 3)	Measure the mean power spectral density over 70/N μs filtered with a square filter of bandwidth equal to the RF bandwidth of the antenna connector or TAB connector centred on the central frequency of the RF bandwidth. 70/N μs average window centre is set from 35/N μs after end of one transmitter ON period + 10 μs to 35/N μs before start of next transmitter ON period – 10 μs. N = SCS/15, where SCS is Sub Carrier Spacing in kHz.
[P1672] 4)	For an antenna connector or TAB connector supporting contiguous CA, measure the mean power spectral density over 70/N μs filtered with a square filter of bandwidth equal to the aggregated BS channel bandwidth BWChannel_CA centred on (Fedge_high+Fedge_low)/2. 70/N μs average window centre is set from 35/N μs after end of one transmitter ON period + 10 μs to 35/N μs before start of next transmitter ON period – 10 μs. N = SCS/15, where SCS is the smallest supported Sub Carrier Spacing in kHz in the aggregated BS channel bandwidth.
[P1673] In addition, for multi-band connector(s), the following steps shall apply:
[P1674] 5)	For multi-band connectors and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved band where single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other band.
[P1675] 6.4.2.5	Test requirements
[P1676] The measured mean power spectral density according to clause 6.4.2.4.2 shall be less than -83 dBm/MHz for carrier frequency f ≤ 3.0 GHz.
[P1677] The measured mean power spectral density according to clause 6.4.2.4.2 shall be less than -82.5 dBm/MHz for carrier frequency 3.0 GHz < f ≤ 7.125 GHz in licensed spectrum only.
[P1678] The measured mean power spectral density according to clause 6.4.2.4.2 shall be less than -82 dBm/MHz for bands n46, n96 and n102.
[P1679] For multi-band connector, the requirement is only applicable during the transmitter OFF period in all supported operating bands.
[P1680] 6.5	Transmitted signal quality
[P1681] 6.5.1	General
[P1682] Unless otherwise stated, the requirements in clause 6.5 apply during the transmitter ON period.
[P1683] 6.5.2	Frequency error
[P1684] 6.5.2.1	Definition and applicability
[P1685] Frequency error is the measure of the difference between the actual BS transmit frequency and the assigned frequency. The same source shall be used for RF frequency and data clock generation.
[P1686] It is not possible to verify by testing that the data clock is derived from the same frequency source as used for RF generation. This may be confirmed by the manufacturer's declaration.
[P1687] For BS type 1-C this requirement shall be applied at the antenna connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1688] For BS type 1-H this requirement shall be applied at each TAB connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1689] 6.5.2.2	Minimum Requirement
[P1690] The minimum requirement is in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.5.1.2.
[P1691] 6.5.2.3	Test purpose
[P1692] The test purpose is to verify that frequency error is within the limit specified by the minimum requirement.
[P1693] 6.5.2.4	Method of test
[P1694] Requirement is tested together with modulation quality test, as described in clause 6.5.3.
[P1695] 6.5.2.5	Test Requirements
[P1696] The modulated carrier frequency of each NR carrier configured by the BS shall be accurate to within the accuracy range given in table 6.5.2.5-1 observed over 1 ms.
[P1697] Table 6.5.2.5-1: Frequency error test requirement
[P1699] The frequency error requirement for NB-IoT is specified in TS 36.141 [24] clause 6.5.1.5.
[P1700] 6.5.3	Modulation quality
[P1701] 6.5.3.1	Definition and applicability
[P1702] Modulation quality is defined by the difference between the measured carrier signal and an ideal signal. Modulation quality can e.g. be expressed as Error Vector Magnitude (EVM). The Error Vector Magnitude is a measure of the difference between the ideal symbols and the measured symbols after the equalization. This difference is called the error vector.
[P1703] For BS type 1-C this requirement shall be applied at the antenna connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1704] For BS type 1-H this requirement shall be applied at each TAB connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1705] 6.5.3.2	Minimum Requirement
[P1706] The minimum requirement is in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.5.2.2.
[P1707] 6.5.3.3	Test purpose
[P1708] The test purpose is to verify that modulation quality is within the limit specified by the minimum requirement.
[P1709] 6.5.3.4	Method of test
[P1710] 6.5.3.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1711] Test environment: Normal; see annex B.2.
[P1712] RF channels to be tested for single carrier: B, M and T; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1713] RF bandwidth positions to be tested for multi-carrier and/or CA:
[P1714] -	BRFBW, MRFBW and TRFBW in single-band operation, see clause 4.9.1;
[P1715] -	BRFBW_T'RFBW and B'RFBW_TRFBW in multi-band operation, see clause 4.9.1.
[P1716] 6.5.3.4.2	Procedure
[P1717] The minimum requirement is applied to all antenna connectors or TAB connectors, they may be tested one at a time or multiple antenna connectors or TAB connectors may be tested in parallel as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C or in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. Whichever method is used the procedure is repeated until all antenna connectors or TAB connectors necessary to demonstrate conformance have been tested.
[P1718] 1)	For an antenna connector or TAB connector declared to be capable of single carrier operation only (D.16), set the antenna connector or the TAB connector under test to transmit a signal according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 using the corresponding test models:
[P1719] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1b if 1024QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1720] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1b at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 1024QAM is supported by BS with power back off, and NR-FR1-TM 3.1a if 256QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1721] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1b at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 1024QAM is supported by BS with power back off and NR-FR1-TM 3.1a at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 256QAM is supported by BS with power back off, and NR-FR1-TM3.1 at maximum power or
[P1722] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1a if 1024QAM is not supported by BS and 256QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1723] -	NR-FR1-TM3.1a at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 256QAM is supported by BS with power back off and 1024QAM is not supported, and NR-FR1-TM3.1 at maximum power, or
[P1724] -	NR-FR1-TM3.1 if highest modulation order supported by BS is 64QAM, or
[P1725] -	NR-FR1-TM3.2 if highest modulation order supported by BS is 16QAM, or
[P1726] -	NR-FR1-TM3.3 if highest modulation order supported by BS is QPSK.
[P1727] For an antenna connector or TAB connector declared to be capable of multi-carrier and/or CA operation (D.15-D.16), set the antenna connector or the TAB connector under test to transmit according to the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8 using the corresponding test models on all carriers configured:
[P1728] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1b if 1024QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1729] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1b at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 1024QAM is supported by BS with power back off, and NR-FR1-TM 3.1a if 256QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1730] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1b at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 1024QAM is supported by BS with power back off and NR-FR1-TM 3.1a at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 256QAM is supported by BS with power back off, and NR-FR1-TM3.1 at maximum power or
[P1731] -	NR-FR1-TM 3.1a if 1024QAM is not supported by BS and 256QAM is supported by BS without power back off, or
[P1732] -	NR-FR1-TM3.1a at manufacturer's declared rated output power if 256QAM is supported by BS with power back off and 1024QAM is not supported, and NR-FR1-TM3.1 at maximum power, or
[P1733] -	NR-FR1-TM3.1 if highest modulation order supported by BS is 64QAM, or
[P1734] -	NR-FR1-TM3.2 if highest modulation order supported by BS is 16QAM, or
[P1735] -	NR-FR1-TM3.3 if highest modulation order supported by BS is QPSK.
[P1736] For NR-FR1-TM3.1a and NR-FR1-TM3.1b, power back-off shall be applied if it is declared.
[P1737] 2)	Measure the EVM and frequency error as defined in annex H.
[P1738] 3)	Repeat steps 1 and 2 for NR-FR1-TM2 if 256QAM and 1024QAM is not supported by BS or for NR-FR1-TM2a if 256QAM is supported by BS and 1024QAM is not supported by BS or for NR-FR1-TM2b if 1024QAM is supported. For NR-FR1-TM2, NR-FR1-TM2a and NR-FR1-TM2b, the OFDM symbol TX power (OSTP) shall be at the lower limit of the dynamic range according to the test procedure in clause 6.3.3.4 and test requirements in clause 6.3.3.5.
[P1739] In addition, for multi-band connector(s), the following steps shall apply:
[P1740] 4)	For multi-band connectors and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved band where single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other band.
[P1741] 6.5.3.5	Test requirements
[P1742] The EVM of each NR carrier for different modulation schemes on PDSCH shall be less than the limits in table 6.5.3.5-1.
[P1743] Table 6.5.3.5-1 EVM requirements for BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H
[P1745] EVM shall be evaluated for each NR carrier over all allocated resource blocks and downlink slots. Different modulation schemes listed in table 6.5.3.5-1 shall be considered for rank 1.
[P1746] For all bandwidths, the EVM measurement shall be performed for each NR carrier over all allocated resource blocks and downlink slots within 10 ms measurement periods. The boundaries of the EVM measurement periods need not be aligned with radio frame boundaries.
[P1747] Table 6.5.3.5-2, 6.5.3.5-3, 6.5.3.5-4 below specify the EVM window length (W) for normal CP for BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H.
[P1748] Table 6.5.3.5-2 EVM window length for normal CP for NR, FR1, 15 kHz SCS
[P1750] Table 6.5.3.5-3 EVM window length for normal CP for NR, FR1, 30 kHz SCS
[P1752] Table 6.5.3.5-4 EVM window length for normal CP for NR, FR1, 60 kHz SCS
[P1754] The modulation quality requirements for NB-IoT are specified in TS 36.141 [24] clause 6.5.2.5.
[P1755] 6.5.4	Time alignment error
[P1756] 6.5.4.1	Definition and applicability
[P1757] This requirement applies to frame timing in MIMO transmission, carrier aggregation and their combinations.
[P1758] Frames of the NR signals present at the BS transmitter antenna connectors or TAB connectors are not perfectly aligned in time and may experience certain timing differences in relation to each other.
[P1759] For BS type 1-C, the TAE is defined as the largest timing difference between any two signals belonging to different antenna connectors for a specific set of signals/transmitter configuration/transmission mode.
[P1760] For BS type 1-H, the TAE is defined as the largest timing difference between any two signals belonging to TAB connectors belonging to different transmitter groups at the transceiver array boundary, where transmitter groups are associated with the TAB connectors in the transceiver unit array corresponding to MIMO transmission, carrier aggregation for a specific set of signals/transmitter configuration/transmission mode.
[P1761] There is no TAE requirement for ATG BS.
[P1762] 6.5.4.2	Minimum requirement
[P1763] The minimum requirements for BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H are in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.5.3.2.
[P1764] 6.5.4.3	Test purpose
[P1765] To verify that the time alignment error is within the limit specified by the minimum requirement.
[P1766] 6.5.4.4	Method of test
[P1767] 6.5.4.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1768] Test environment: Normal, see annex B.2.
[P1769] RF channels to be tested for single carrier:	M; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1770] RF bandwidth positions to be tested for multi-carrier and/or CA:
[P1771] -	MRFBW in single-band operation, see clause 4.9.1.
[P1772] -	BRFBW_T'RFBW and B'RFBW_TRFBW in multi-band operation, see clause 4.9.1.
[P1773] 6.5.4.4.2	Procedure
[P1774] For BS type 1-C antenna connectors to be tested are for a specific set of signals/transmitter configuration/transmission mode.
[P1775] For BS type 1-H TAB connectors to be tested are identified from the declared sets of TAB connector beam forming groups in the TAE groups declaration (D.31).
[P1776] Compliance is to be demonstrated between all pairs of single-band connectors and/or multi-band connectors, however it is not required to exhaustively measure TAE between every combination of pairs of representative connectors. Compliance can be demonstrated by comparison of a reduced set of representative measurement results.
[P1777] 1)	Conducted measurement setup:
[P1778] - For BS type 1-C: Connect two antenna connectors to the measurement equipment according to annex D.1.3. Terminate any unused antenna connector(s).
[P1779] - For BS type 1-H: Connect two representative TAB connectors one from separate TAE group (D.31) to the measurement equipment according to annex D.3.4. Terminate any unused TAB connector(s).
[P1780] 2)	Set the connectors under test to transmit NR-FR1-TM 1.1 or any DL signal using MIMO transmission or carrier aggregation.
[P1781] NOTE:	For MIMO transmission, different ports may be configured in NR-FR1-TM 1.1 (using PDSCH DMRS ports 1000 and 1001).
[P1782] 3)	For a connectors declared to be capable of single carrier operation only (D.16), set the representative connectors under test to transmit according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 using the corresponding test models in clause 4.9.2 at rated carrier output power (Prated,c,AC, or Prated,c,TABC, D.21).
[P1783] If the connector under test supports intra band contiguous or non-contiguous CA, set the representative connectors to transmit using the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8.
[P1784] If the BS supports inter band CA, set the representative connectors to transmit, for each band, a single carrier or all carriers, using the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8.
[P1785] For a connector declared to be capable of multi-carrier operation (D.15), set the BS to transmit according to the applicable test signal configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8 using the corresponding test model in clause 4.9.2 on all carriers configured.
[P1786] 4)	Measure the time alignment error between the different PDSCH demodulation reference signals on different antenna ports belonging to different connectorson the carrier(s) from the representative connectors under test.
[P1787] 5)	Repeat step 1 - 4 for any other configuration of connectors, which could be required to demonstrate compliance.
[P1788] In addition, for multi-band connectors, the following steps shall apply:
[P1789] 6)	For a multi-band connectors and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved operating band where single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other operating band.
[P1790] 6.5.4.5	Test requirement
[P1791] For MIMO transmission, at each carrier frequency, TAE shall not exceed 90 ns.
[P1792] For intra-band contiguous CA, with or without MIMO, TAE shall not exceed 285 ns.
[P1793] For intra-band non-contiguous CA, with or without MIMO, TAE shall not exceed 3.025 µs.
[P1794] For inter-band CA, with or without MIMO, TAE shall not exceed 3.025 µs.
[P1795] The time alignment error requirements for NB-IoT are specified in TS 36.141 [24] clause 6.5.3.5.
[P1796] 6.6	Unwanted emissions
[P1797] 6.6.1	General
[P1798] Unwanted emissions consist of out-of-band emissions and spurious emissions according to ITU definitions in recommendation ITU-R SM.329 [5]. In ITU terminology, out of band emissions are unwanted emissions immediately outside the channel bandwidth resulting from the modulation process and non-linearity in the transmitter but excluding spurious emissions. Spurious emissions are emissions which are caused by unwanted transmitter effects such as harmonics emission, parasitic emission, intermodulation products and frequency conversion products, but exclude out of band emissions.
[P1799] The out-of-band emissions requirement for the BS transmitter is specified both in terms of Adjacent Channel Leakage power Ratio (ACLR) and operating band unwanted emissions (OBUE).
[P1800] The maximum offset of the operating band unwanted emissions mask from the operating band edge is ΔfOBUE. The operating band unwanted emissions define all unwanted emissions in each supported downlink operating band plus the frequency ranges ΔfOBUE above and ΔfOBUE below each band. Unwanted emissions outside of this frequency range are limited by a spurious emissions requirement.
[P1801] The values of ΔfOBUE are defined in table 6.6.1-1 for the NR operating bands.
[P1802] Table 6.6.1-1: Maximum offset of OBUE outside the downlink operating band
[P1804] For band n46, n96 and n102, the values of ΔfOBUE are defined in table 6.6.1-1a.
[P1805] Table 6.6.1-1a: Maximum offset of OBUE outside the downlink operating band
[P1807] For band n104, the values of ΔfOBUE are defined in table 6.6.1-1b.
[P1809] Table 6.6.1-1b: Maximum offset of OBUE outside the downlink operating band for band n104
[P1810] For BS type 1-H the unwanted emission requirements are applied per the TAB connector TX min cell groups for all the configurations supported by the BS. The basic limits and corresponding emissions scaling are defined in each relevant clause.
[P1811] There is in addition a requirement for occupied bandwidth.
[P1812] 6.6.2	Occupied bandwidth
[P1813] 6.6.2.1	Definition and applicability
[P1814] The occupied bandwidth is the width of a frequency band such that, below the lower and above the upper frequency limits, the mean powers emitted are each equal to a specified percentage /2 of the total mean transmitted power. See also Recommendation ITU-R SM.328 [12].
[P1815] The value of /2 shall be taken as 0.5%.
[P1816] The occupied bandwidth requirement shall apply during the transmitter ON period for a single transmitted carrier. The minimum requirement below may be applied regionally. There may also be regional requirements to declare the occupied bandwidth according to the definition in the present clause.
[P1817] For BS type 1-C this requirement shall be applied at the antenna connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1818] For BS type 1-H this requirement shall be appliedat each TAB connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1819] 6.6.2.2	Minimum Requirements
[P1820] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H is in TS 38.104 [2] clause 6.6.2
[P1821] 6.6.2.3	Test purpose
[P1822] The test purpose is to verify that the emission at the antenna connector or TAB connector does not occupy an excessive bandwidth for the service to be provided and is, therefore, not likely to create interference to other users of the spectrum beyond undue limits.
[P1823] 6.6.2.4	Method of test
[P1824] 6.6.2.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1825] Test environment: Normal; see annex B.2.
[P1826] RF channels to be tested for single carrier: M; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1827] Aggregated BS channel bandwidth positions to be tested for contiguous carrier aggregation: MBW Channel CA; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1828] 1)	Connect the measurement device to the BS antenna connector or TAB connector as shown in annex D1.1 for BS type 1-C or D3.1 for BS type 1-H.
[P1829] 2)	For a BS declared to be capable of single carrier operation (D.16), start transmission according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 using the corresponding test model NR-FR1-TM1.1 at manufacturer's declared rated output power (Prated,c,AC, or Prated,c,TABC, D.21).
[P1830] For a BS declared to be capable of contiguous CA operation, set the BS to transmit according to NR-FR1-TM1.1 on all carriers configured using the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7.4 and 4.8.
[P1831] For a BS declared to be capable of NB-IoT operation in NR in-band (D.41), test shall be performed using N-TM according to clause 4.9.2.2.9.
[P1832] 6.6.2.4.2	Procedure
[P1833] 1)	Measure the spectrum emission of the transmitted signal using at least the number of measurement points, and across a span, as listed in table 6.6.2.4.2-1. The selected resolution bandwidth (RBW) filter of the analyser shall be 30 kHz or less.
[P1834] Table 6.6.2.4.2-1: Span and number of measurement points for OBW measurements
[P1836] NOTE:	The detection mode of the spectrum analyzer will not have any effect on the result if the statistical properties of the out-of-OBW power are the same as those of the inside-OBW power. Both are expected to have the Rayleigh distribution of the amplitude of Gaussian noise. In any case where the statistics are not the same, though, the detection mode must be power responding. The analyser may be set to respond to the average of the power (root-mean-square of the voltage) across the measurement cell.
[P1837] 2)	Compute the total of the power, P0, (in power units, not decibel units) of all the measurement cells in the measurement span. Compute P1, the power outside the occupied bandwidth on each side. P1 is half of the total power outside the bandwidth. P1 is half of (100 % - (occupied percentage)) of P0. For the occupied percentage of 99 %, P1 is 0.005 times P0.
[P1838] 3)	Determine the lowest frequency, f1, for which the sum of all power in the measurement cells from the beginning of the span to f1 exceeds P1.
[P1839] 4)	Determine the highest frequency, f2, for which the sum of all power in the measurement cells from f2 to the end of the span exceeds P1.
[P1840] 5)	Compute the occupied bandwidth as f2 - f1.
[P1841] In addition, for a multi-band capable BS, the following step shall apply:
[P1842] 6)	For multi-band capable BS and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved band where single carrier test models shall apply, with no carrier activated in the other band. In addition, when contiguous CA is supported, single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other band.
[P1843] 6.6.2.5	Test requirements
[P1844] The occupied bandwidth for each carrier shall be less than the channel bandwidth as defined in TS 38.104 [2], table 5.3.5-1 for BS type 1-C and BS type 1-H. For contiguous CA, the occupied bandwidth shall be less than or equal to the aggregated BS channel bandwidth as defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 5.3A.
[P1845] 6.6.3	Adjacent Channel Leakage Power Ratio (ACLR)
[P1846] 6.6.3.1	Definition and applicability
[P1847] Adjacent Channel Leakage power Ratio (ACLR) is the ratio of the filtered mean power centred on the assigned channel frequency to the filtered mean power centred on an adjacent channel frequency.
[P1848] The requirements shall apply outside the Base Station RF Bandwidth or Radio Bandwidth whatever the type of transmitter considered (single carrier or multi-carrier) and for all transmission modes foreseen by the manufacturer's specification.
[P1849] The requirements shall also apply if the BS supports NB-IoT operation in NR in-band.
[P1850] For a BS operating in non-contiguous spectrum, the ACLR or the CACLR requirement in Tables  6.6.3.5.2-3 to 6.6.3.5.2.4a shall apply in sub block gaps, depending on the operating band and the sub-block gap size (Wgap) where the limit applies.
[P1851] For a multi-band connector, the ACLR or the CACLR requirement in Tables  6.6.3.5.2-3 to 6.6.3.5.2.4a shall apply in Inter RF Bandwidth gaps, depending on the operating band and the Inter RF Bandwidth gap size (Wgap) where the limit applies.
[P1852] The requirement applies during the transmitter ON period.
[P1853] 6.6.3.2	Minimum requirement
[P1854] The minimum requirement applies per single-band connector, or per multi-band connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1855] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C is defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.6.3.3.
[P1856] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-H is defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.6.3.4.
[P1857] 6.6.3.3	Test purpose
[P1858] To verify that the adjacent channel leakage power ratio requirement shall be met as specified by the minimum requirement.
[P1859] 6.6.3.4	Method of test
[P1860] 6.6.3.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1861] Test environment: Normal; see annex B.2.
[P1862] RF channels to be tested for single carrier: B, M and T; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1863] Base Station RF Bandwidth positions to be tested for multi-carrier and/or CA:
[P1864] -	BRFBW, MRFBW and TRFBW in single-band operation; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1865] -	BRFBW_T'RFBW and B'RFBW_TRFBW in multi-band operation, see clause 4.9.1.
[P1866] 6.6.3.4.2	Procedure
[P1867] For BS type 1-H where there may be multiple TAB connectors, they may be tested one at a time or multiple TAB connectors may be tested in parallel as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C or in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. Whichever method is used the procedure is repeated until all TAB connectors necessary to demonstrate conformance have been tested.
[P1868] 1)	Connect the single-band connector or multi-band connector under test to measurement equipment as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C and in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. All connectors not under test shall be terminated.
[P1869] The measurement device characteristics shall be:
[P1870] -	Measurement filter bandwidth: defined in clause 6.6.3.5.
[P1871] -	Detection mode: true RMS voltage or true average power.
[P1872] The emission power should be averaged over an appropriate time duration to ensure the measurement is within the measurement uncertainty in Table 4.1.2.2-1.
[P1873] 2)	For a connectors declared to be capable of single carrier operation only (D.16), set the representative connectors under test to transmit according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 using the corresponding test models NR-FR1-TM 1.1 in clause 4.9.2 at rated carrier output power Prated,c,AC for BS type 1-C and Prated,c,TABC for BS type 1-H (D.21).
[P1874] For a connector under test declared to be capable of multi-carrier and/or CA operation (D.15-D.16) set the connector under test to transmit on all carriers configured using the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8 using the corresponding test models or set of physical channels in clause 4.9.2.
[P1875] For a BS declared to be capable of NB-IoT operation in NR in-band (D.41), test shall be performed using N-TM according to clause 4.9.2.2.9.
[P1876] 3)	Measure ACLR for the frequency offsets both side of channel frequency as specified in table 6.6.3.5.2-1. In multiple carrier case only offset frequencies below the lowest and above the highest carrier frequency used shall be measured.
[P1877] 4)	For the ACLR requirement applied inside sub-block gap for non-contiguous spectrum operation, or inside Inter RF Bandwidth gap for multi-band operation:
[P1878] a)	Measure ACLR inside sub-block gap or Inter RF Bandwidth gap as specified in clause 6.6.3.5.2, if applicable.
[P1879] b)	Measure CACLR inside sub-block gap or Inter RF Bandwidth gap as specified in clause 6.6.3.5.2, if applicable.
[P1880] 5)	Repeat the test with the channel set-up according to NR-FR1-TM 1.2 in clause 4.9.2.
[P1881] In addition, for multi-band connectors, the following steps shall apply:
[P1882] 6)	For a multi-band connectors and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved operating band where single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other operating band.
[P1883] 6.6.3.5	Test requirements
[P1884] 6.6.3.5.1	General requirements
[P1885] The ACLR requirements in clause 6.6.3.5.2 shall apply as described in clauses 6.6.3.5.3 or 6.6.3.5.4.
[P1886] 6.6.3.5.2	Limits and basic limits
[P1887] The ACLR is defined with a square filter of bandwidth equal to the transmission bandwidth configuration of the transmitted signal (BWConfig) centred on the assigned channel frequency and a filter centred on the adjacent channel frequency according to the tables below.
[P1888] For operation in paired and unpaired spectrum except for band n46, n96 and n102, the ACLR shall be higher than the value specified in table 6.6.3.5.2-1.
[P1889] Table 6.6.3.5.2-1: Base station ACLR limit
[P1891] For band n46, n96 and n102, the ACLR shall be higher than the value specified in Table 6.6.3.5.2-1a.
[P1892] Table 6.6.3.5.2-1a: Base station ACLR limit for band n46, n96 and n102
[P1894] The ACLR absolute basic limit is specified in table 6.6.3.5.2-2.
[P1895] Table 6.6.3.5.2-2: Base station ACLR absolute basic limit
[P1897] For operation in non-contiguous spectrum or multiple bands except for band n46, n96 and n102, the ACLR shall be higher than the value specified in table 6.6.3.5.2-3.
[P1898] Table 6.6.3.5.2-3: Base Station ACLR limit in non-contiguous spectrum or multiple bands
[P1900] For operation in non-contiguous spectrum for band n46, n96 and n102, the ACLR shall be higher than the value specified in Table 6.6.3.2-3a.
[P1901] Table 6.6.3.5.2-3a: Base Station ACLR limit in non-contiguous spectrum for band n46, n96 and n102
[P1903] The Cumulative Adjacent Channel Leakage power Ratio (CACLR) in a sub-block gap or the Inter RF Bandwidth gap is the ratio of:
[P1904] a)	the sum of the filtered mean power centred on the assigned channel frequencies for the two carriers adjacent to each side of the sub-block gap or the Inter RF Bandwidth gap, and
[P1905] b)	the filtered mean power centred on a frequency channel adjacent to one of the respective sub-block edges or Base Station RF Bandwidth edges.
[P1906] The assumed filter for the adjacent channel frequency is defined in table 6.6.3.5.2-4 and the filters on the assigned channels are defined in table 6.6.3.5.2-6.
[P1907] For operation in non-contiguous spectrum or multiple bands except for band n46, n96 and n102, the CACLR for NR carriers located on either side of the sub-block gap or the Inter RF Bandwidth gap shall be higher than the value specified in table 6.6.3.5.2-4.
[P1908] Table 6.6.3.5.2-4: Base station CACLR limit
[P1910] For operation in non-contiguous spectrum for band n46, n96 and n102, the CACLR for NR carriers located on either side of the sub-block gap shall be higher than the value specified in Table 6.6.3.5.2-4a.
[P1911] Table 6.6.3.5.2-4a: Base Station CACLR limit for band n46, n96 and n102
[P1913] The CACLR absolute basic limit is specified in table 6.6.3.5.2-5.
[P1914] Table 6.6.3.5.2-5: Base station CACLR absolute basic limit
[P1916] Table 6.6.3.5.2-6: Filter parameters for the assigned channel
[P1918] 6.6.3.5.3	BS type 1-C
[P1919] The ACLR test requirements for BS type 1-C are given in table 6.6.3.5.2-1 or 6.6.3.5.2-3 applies per antenna connector. Conformance can be shown by meeting the ACLR limit in table 6.6.3.5.2-1 or 6.6.3.5.2-3, or the absolute basic limits in table 6.6.3.5.2-2, whichever is less stringent.
[P1920] The CACLR test requirements for BS type 1-C are given in table 6.6.3.5.2-4 applies per antenna connector. Conformance can be shown by meeting the CACLR limit in table 6.6.3.5.2-4 or the absolute basic limits in table 6.6.3.5.2-5, whichever is less stringent.
[P1921] For Band n41 and n90 operation in Japan, absolute ACLR limits shall be applied to the sum of the absolute ACLR power over all antenna connectors for BS type 1-C.
[P1922] 6.6.3.5.4	BS type 1-H
[P1923] The ACLR absolute basic limits in table 6.6.3.5.2-2+ X (where X = 10log10(NTXU,countedpercell)) or the ACLR limits in table 6.6.3.5.2-1, or 6.6.3.5.2-3, whichever is less stringent, shall apply for each TAB connector TX min cell group.
[P1924] The CACLR absolute basic limits in table 6.6.3.5.2-5 + X, (where X = 10log10(NTXU,countedpercell)) or the CACLR limits in table 6.6.3.5.2-4, whichever is less stringent, shall apply for each TAB connector TX min cell group.
[P1925] Conformance to the BS type 1-H ACLR (CACLR) limit can be demonstrated by meeting at least one of the following criteria as determined by the manufacturer
[P1926] 1)	The ratio of the sum of the filtered mean power measured on each TAB connector in the TAB connector TX min cell group at the assigned channel frequency to the sum of the filtered mean power measured on each TAB connector in the TAB connector TX min cell group at the adjacent channel frequency shall be greater than or equal to the ACLR (CACLR) limit of the BS. This shall apply for each TAB connector TX min cell group.
[P1927] Or
[P1928] 2)	The ratio of the filtered mean power at the TAB connector centred on the assigned channel frequency to the filtered mean power at this TAB connector centred on the adjacent channel frequency shall be greater than or equal to the ACLR (CACLR) limit of the BS for every TAB connector in the TAB connector TX min cell group, for each TAB connector TX min cell group.
[P1929] In case the ACLR (CACLR) absolute basic limit of BS type 1-H are applied, the conformance can be demonstrated by meeting at least one of the following criteria as determined by the manufacturer:
[P1930] 1)	The sum of the filtered mean power measured on each TAB connector in the TAB connector TX min cell group at the adjacent channel frequency shall be less than or equal to the ACLR (CACLR) absolute basic limit + X (where X = 10log10(NTXU,countedpercell)) of the BS. This shall apply to each TAB connector TX min cell group.
[P1931] Or
[P1932] 2)	The filtered mean power at each TAB connector centred on the adjacent channel frequency shall be less than or equal to the ACLR (CACLR) absolute basic limit of the BS scaled by X -10log10(n) for every TAB connector in the TAB connector TX min cell group, for each TAB connector TX min cell group, where n is the number of TAB connectors in the TAB connector TX min cell group.
[P1933] 6.6.4	Operating band unwanted emissions
[P1934] 6.6.4.1	Definition and applicability
[P1935] Unless otherwise stated, the operating band unwanted emission (OBUE) limits in FR1 are defined from ΔfOBUE below the lowest frequency of each supported downlink operating band up to ΔfOBUE above the highest frequency of each supported downlink operating band. The values of ΔfOBUE are defined in table 6.6.1-1 for the NR operating bands.
[P1936] The requirements shall apply whatever the type of transmitter considered and for all transmission modes foreseen by the manufacturer's specification. In addition, for a BS operating in non-contiguous spectrum, the requirements apply inside any sub-block gap. In addition, for a BS operating in multiple bands, the requirements apply inside any Inter RF Bandwidth gap.
[P1937] Basic limits are specified in the tables below, where:
[P1938] -	f is the separation between the channel edge frequency and the nominal -3 dB point of the measuring filter closest to the carrier frequency.
[P1939] -	f_offset is the separation between the channel edge frequency and the centre of the measuring filter.
[P1940] -	f_offsetmax is the offset to the frequency ΔfOBUE outside the downlink operating band, where ΔfOBUE is defined in table 6.6.1-1.
[P1941] -	fmax is equal to f_offsetmax minus half of the bandwidth of the measuring filter.
[P1942] For a multi-band connector inside any Inter RF Bandwidth gaps with Wgap < 2*ΔfOBUE, a combined basic limit shall be applied which is the cumulative sum of the basic limits specified at the Base Station RF Bandwidth edges on each side of the Inter RF Bandwidth gap. The basic limit for Base Station RF Bandwidth edge is specified in the clauses 6.6.4.5.2 to 6.6.4.5.5below, where in this case:
[P1943] -	f is the separation between the Base Station RF Bandwidth edge frequency and the nominal -3 dB point of the measuring filter closest to the Base Station RF Bandwidth edge.
[P1944] -	f_offset is the separation between the Base Station RF Bandwidth edge frequency and the centre of the measuring filter.
[P1945] -	f_offsetmax is equal to the Inter RF Bandwidth gap minus half of the bandwidth of the measuring filter.
[P1946] -	fmax is equal to f_offsetmax minus half of the bandwidth of the measuring filter.
[P1947] For a multi-band connector, the operating band unwanted emission basic limits apply also in a supported operating band without any carrier transmitted, in the case where there are carrier(s) transmitted in another supported operating band. In this case, no cumulative basic limit is applied in the inter-band gap between a supported downlink operating band with carrier(s) transmitted and a supported downlink operating band without any carrier transmitted and
[P1948] -	In case the inter-band gap between a supported downlink operating band with carrier(s) transmitted and a supported downlink operating band without any carrier transmitted is less than 2*ΔfOBUE, f_offsetmax shall be the offset to the frequency ΔfOBUE MHz outside the outermost edges of the two supported downlink operating bands and the operating band unwanted emission basic limit of the band where there are carriers transmitted, as defined in the tables of the present clause, shall apply across both downlink bands.
[P1949] -	In other cases, the operating band unwanted emission basic limits of the band where there are carriers transmitted, as defined in the tables of the present clause for the largest frequency offset (fmax), shall apply from ΔfOBUE MHz below the lowest frequency, up to ΔfOBUE MHz above the highest frequency of the supported downlink operating band without any carrier transmitted.
[P1950] For a multicarrier single-band connector or a single-band connector configured for intra-band contiguous or non-contiguous carrier aggregation the definitions above apply to the lower edge of the carrier transmitted at the lowest carrier frequency and the upper edge of the carrier transmitted at the highest carrier frequency within a specified frequency band.
[P1951] In addition inside any sub-block gap for a single-band connector operating in non-contiguous spectrum, a combined basic limit shall be applied which is the cumulative sum of the basic limits specified for the adjacent sub blocks on each side of the sub block gap. The basic limit for each sub block is specified in the subcluases 6.6.4.5.2 to 6.6.4.5.5below, where in this case:
[P1952] -	f is the separation between the sub block edge frequency and the nominal -3 dB point of the measuring filter closest to the sub block edge.
[P1953] -	f_offset is the separation between the sub block edge frequency and the centre of the measuring filter.
[P1954] -	f_offsetmax is equal to the sub block gap bandwidth minus half of the bandwidth of the measuring filter.
[P1955] -	fmax is equal to f_offsetmax minus half of the bandwidth of the measuring filter.
[P1956] For Wide Area BS, the requirements of either clause 6.6.4.5.2 (Category A limits) or clause 6.6.4.5.3 (Category B limits) shall apply.
[P1957] For Medium Range BS, the requirements in clause 6.6.4.5.4 shall apply (Category A and B).
[P1958] For Local Area BS, the requirements of clause 6.6.4.5.5 shall apply (Category A and B).
[P1959] The requirements shall also apply if the BS supports NB-IoT operation in NR in-band.
[P1960] The application of either Category A or Category B basic limits shall be the same as for transmitter spurious emissions in clause 6.6.5.
[P1961] 6.6.4.2	Minimum requirement
[P1962] The minimum requirement applies per single-band connector, or per multi-band connector supporting transmission in the operating band.
[P1963] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-C is defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.6.4.3.
[P1964] The minimum requirement for BS type 1-H is defined in TS 38.104 [2], clause 6.6.4.4.
[P1965] 6.6.4.3	Test purpose
[P1966] This test measures the emissions close to the assigned channel bandwidth of the wanted signal, while the transmitter is in operation.
[P1967] 6.6.4.4	Method of test
[P1968] 6.6.4.4.1	Initial conditions
[P1969] Test environment: Normal; see annex B.2.
[P1970] RF channels to be tested for single carrier: B, M and T; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1971] Base Station RF Bandwidth positions to be tested for multi-carrier:
[P1972] -	BRFBW, MRFBW and TRFBW in single-band operation; see clause 4.9.1.
[P1973] -	BRFBW_T'RFBW and B'RFBW_TRFBW in multi-band operation, see clause 4.9.1.
[P1974] 6.6.4.4.2	Procedure
[P1975] For BS type 1-H where there may be multiple TAB connectors, they may be tested one at a time or multiple TAB connectors may be tested in parallel as shown in annex D.3.1. Whichever method is used the procedure is repeated until all TAB connectors necessary to demonstrate conformance have been tested.
[P1976] 1)	Connect the single-band connector or multi-band connector under test to measurement equipment as shown in annex D.1.1 for BS type 1-C or in annex D.3.1 for BS type 1-H. All connectors not under test shall be terminated.
[P1977] As a general rule, the resolution bandwidth of the measuring equipment should be equal to the measurement bandwidth. However, to improve measurement accuracy, sensitivity, efficiency and avoiding e.g. carrier leakage, the resolution bandwidth may be smaller than the measurement bandwidth. When the resolution bandwidth is smaller than the measurement bandwidth, the result should be integrated over the measurement bandwidth in order to obtain the equivalent noise bandwidth of the measurement bandwidth.
[P1978] The measurement device characteristics shall be:
[P1979] -	Detection mode: True RMS.
[P1980] The emission power should be averaged over an appropriate time duration to ensure the measurement is within the measurement uncertainty in Table 4.1.2.2-1.
[P1981] 2)	For a connectors declared to be capable of single carrier operation only, set the representative connectors under test to transmit according to the applicable test configuration in clause 4.8 at rated carrier output power Prated,c,AC for BS type 1-C and Prated,c,TABC for BS type 1-H (D.21). Channel set-up shall be according to NR-FR1-TM 1.1.
[P1982] For a connector under test declared to be capable of multi-carrier and/or CA operation set the connector under test to transmit on all carriers configured using the applicable test configuration and corresponding power setting specified in clauses 4.7 and 4.8 using the corresponding test models or set of physical channels in clause 4.9.2.
[P1983] For a BS declared to be capable of NB-IoT operation in NR in-band (D.41), test shall be performed using N-TM according to clause 4.9.2.2.9.
[P1984] 3)	Step the centre frequency of the measurement filter in contiguous steps and measure the emission within the specified frequency ranges with the specified measurement bandwidth. For connector under test declared to operate in multiple bands or non-contiguous spectrum, the emission within the Inter RF Bandwidth or sub-block gap shall be measured using the specified measurement bandwidth from the closest RF Bandwidth or sub block edge.
[P1985] 4)	Repeat the test for the remaining test cases, with the channel set-up according to NR-FR1-TM 1.2.
[P1986] In addition, for multi-band connectors, the following steps shall apply:
[P1987] 5)	For a multi-band connectors and single band tests, repeat the steps above per involved operating band where single band test configurations and test models shall apply with no carrier activated in the other operating band.

The additional spectrum emission mask is applied to the total bandwidth of the remaining transmitted channels.

[P2088] 6.6.4.5.6	Basic limits for additional requirements
[P2089] 6.6.4.5.6.1	Limits in FCC Title 47
[P2090] In addition to the requirements in clauses 6.6.4.5.2 to 6.6.4.5.5, the BS may have to comply with the applicable emission limits established by FCC Title 47 [13], when deployed in regions where those limits are applied, and under the conditions declared by the manufacturer.
[P2091] 6.6.4.5.6.2	Protection of DTT
[P2092] In certain regions the following requirement may apply for protection of DTT. For BS type 1-C or BS type 1-H operating in Band n20, the level of emissions in the band 470-790 MHz, measured in an 8 MHz filter bandwidth on centre frequencies Ffilter according to table 6.6.4.5.6.2-1, basic limit is PEM,N declared by the manufacturer. This requirement applies in the frequency range 470-790 MHz even though part of the range falls in the spurious domain.
[P2093] Table 6.6.4.5.6.2-1: Declared emissions basic limit for protection of DTT
[P2095] Note:	The regional requirement is defined in terms of EIRP (effective isotropic radiated power), which is dependent on both the BS emissions at the antenna connector and the deployment (including antenna gain and feeder loss). The requirement defined above provides the characteristics of the BS needed to verify compliance with the regional requirement. Compliance with the regional requirement can be determined using the method outlined in TS 36.104 [22], annex G.

However, to improve measurement accuracy, sensitivity and efficiency, the resolution bandwidth may be smaller than the measurement bandwidth. When the resolution bandwidth is smaller than the measurement bandwidth, the result should be integrated over the measurement bandwidth in order to obtain the equivalent noise bandwidth of the measurement bandwidth.
